Prior to Hearst, Cathie headed the Newspaper Association of America (now the News Media Alliance) and served as president and publisher of <em>USA Today</em>, turning it into the #1 newspaper in the country. She has sat on many company boards, including Coca-Cola, IBM, Gannett Co., PubMatic, and Hearst. Cathie is a member of the Council on Foreign Relations, author of the best-selling book <em>Basic Black</em>, and has appeared on Forbes and Fortune’s lists of the most powerful women in business on numerous occasions.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div><div class="AccordionItemContainer-sc-1qjtw83-0 hXAjhx"><button class="AccordionItemHeader-sc-1qjtw83-1 dInLDr"><div class="AccordionItemLabel-sc-1qjtw83-2 eLFFEv"><p class="Heading3-sc-1qqpy3q-2 iYSWwx"><span aria-label="Mike Perlis" class="BaseSplitTextWrapper-bm6yvl-0 SplitTextWrapper-bm6yvl-1 eWoTJK bYZVRj">Mike Perlis</span></p></div><div class="AccordionItemIcon-sc-1qjtw83-3 kIyaXu"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" style="transform:rotate(0deg)" width="20" height="20"><path fill-rule="evenodd" clip-rule="evenodd" d="M20 10.5001H0V9.50012H20V10.5001Z" fill="currentColor" opacity="1"></path><path fill-rule="evenodd" clip-rule="evenodd" d="M9.5 20L9.5 -4.37114e-08L10.5 0L10.5 20L9.5 20Z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg></div></button><div class="AccordionMain-oouz1x-0 ktcGdI"><div aria-hidden="true" class="AccordionContent-oouz1x-1 bECkKn"><div class="Advisor-sc-1wajnin-0 cusZoM"><div class="RichTextMain-sc-1ttl6gc-0 gOdoUF"><p class="TextBodyAdvisor-sc-1qqpy3q-6 bhkkLL"><span aria-label="Mike is a media industry executive and innovator with deep experience leading a wide range of content and multimedia brands. He was the first non-family member to run Forbes Media as CEO and spearheaded its shift towards digital and technology products, resulting in the sale of its majority interest to Hong Kong-based investors in 2014. Prior to Forbes, Mike was a general partner at SoftBank Capital for 10 years, where he specialized in digital content startups with notable investments which include BuzzFeed, HuffPost (sold to AOL), GSI Commerce (sold to eBay), Associated Content (sold to Yahoo!). Mike currently serves on the boards of Condé Nast, IDG, and the Newhouse School of Public Communications at Syracuse University." class="BaseSplitTextWrapper-bm6yvl-0 SplitTextWrapper-bm6yvl-1 eWoTJK cIVJxa">Mike is a media industry executive and innovator with deep experience leading a wide range of content and multimedia brands. He was the first non-family member to run Forbes Media as CEO and spearheaded its shift towards digital and technology products, resulting in the sale of its majority interest to Hong Kong-based investors in 2014. Prior to Forbes, Mike was a general partner at SoftBank Capital for 10 years, where he specialized in digital content startups with notable investments which include BuzzFeed, HuffPost (sold to AOL), GSI Commerce (sold to eBay), Associated Content (sold to Yahoo!). Before joining SoftBank, Mike served as president and CEO of Ziff Davis Publishing until its sale in 2000. Earlier in his career, he ran several publications, including <em>GQ</em>, <em>Playboy</em>, <em>Runner’s World</em>, and <em>Men’s Health</em>. Mike currently serves on the boards of Condé Nast, IDG, and the Newhouse School of Public Communications at Syracuse University.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div value="50" class="SpacerVHMain-x8s4h8-0 cAixNX"></div><div data-scroll-section="true"
